1. //*********************************************************
2. //
3. // Copyright (c) Microsoft. All rights reserved.
4. // This code is licensed under the MIT License (MIT).
5. // THIS CODE IS PROVIDED *AS IS* WITHOUT WARRANTY OF

11. 
12. using SDKTemplate;
13. using System;
14. using System.Threading.Tasks;
15. using Windows.Media.Audio;
16. using Windows.Media.Render;
17. using Windows.Storage;
18. using Windows.Storage.Pickers;
19. using Windows.UI;
20. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml;
21. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Controls;
22. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Controls.Primitives;
23. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Media;
24. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Navigation;
25. 
26. // The Blank Page item template is documented at http://go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkId=234238
27. 
28. namespace AudioCreation
29. {
30.     /// <summary>
31.     /// Using the AudioGraph API to playback from a file input.
32.     /// </summary>
33.     public sealed partial class Scenario1_FilePlayback : Page
34.     {
35.         private MainPage rootPage;
36. 
37.         private AudioGraph graph;
38.         private AudioFileInputNode fileInput;
39.         private AudioDeviceOutputNode deviceOutput;
40. 
41.         public Scenario1_FilePlayback()
42.         {
43.             this.InitializeComponent();
44.         }
45. 
46.         protected override async void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)
47.         {
48.             rootPage = MainPage.Current;
49.             await CreateAudioGraph();
50.         }
51. 
52.         protected override void OnNavigatedFrom(NavigationEventArgs e)
53.         {
54.             // Destroy the graph if the page is naviated away from
55.             if (graph != null)
56.             {
57.                 graph.Dispose();
58.             }
59.         }
60. 
61.         private async void File_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
62.         {
63.             // Clear any old messages.
64.             rootPage.NotifyUser("", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
65. 
66.             // If another file is already loaded into the FileInput node
67.             if (fileInput != null)
68.             {
69.                 // Release the file and dispose the contents of the node
70.                 fileInput.Dispose();
71.                 // Stop playback since a new file is being loaded. Also reset the button UI
72.                 if (graphButton.Content.Equals("Stop Graph"))
73.                 {
74.                     TogglePlay();
75.                 }
76.             }
77. 
78.             // TODO[migrate-001]: see PATTERNS.md#pickers
79.             FileOpenPicker filePicker = new FileOpenPicker();
80.             filePicker.SuggestedStartLocation = PickerLocationId.MusicLibrary;
81.             filePicker.FileTypeFilter.Add(".mp3");
82.             filePicker.FileTypeFilter.Add(".wav");
83.             filePicker.FileTypeFilter.Add(".wma");
84.             filePicker.FileTypeFilter.Add(".m4a");
85.             filePicker.ViewMode = PickerViewMode.Thumbnail;
86.             StorageFile file = await filePicker.PickSingleFileAsync();
87. 
88.             // File can be null if cancel is hit in the file picker
89.             if(file == null)
90.             {
91.                 return;
92.             }
93. 
94.             CreateAudioFileInputNodeResult fileInputResult = await graph.CreateFileInputNodeAsync(file);
95.             if (AudioFileNodeCreationStatus.Success != fileInputResult.Status)
96.             {
97.                 // Cannot read input file
98.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("Cannot read input file because {0}", fileInputResult.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
99.                 return;
100.             }
101. 
102.             fileInput = fileInputResult.FileInputNode;
103. 
104.             if (fileInput.Duration <= TimeSpan.FromSeconds(3))
105.             {
106.                 // Imported file is too short
107.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("Please pick an audio file which is longer than 3 seconds", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
108.                 fileInput.Dispose();
109.                 fileInput = null;
110.                 return;
111.             }
112. 
113.             fileInput.AddOutgoingConnection(deviceOutput);
114.             fileButton.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Green);
115. 
116.             // Trim the file: set the start time to 3 seconds from the beginning
117.             // fileInput.EndTime can be used to trim from the end of file
118.             fileInput.StartTime = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(3);
119. 
120.             // Enable buttons in UI to start graph, loop and change playback speed factor
121.             graphButton.IsEnabled = true;
122.             loopToggle.IsEnabled = true;
123.             playSpeedSlider.IsEnabled = true;
124.         }
125. 
126.         private void Graph_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
127.         {
128.             TogglePlay();
129.         }
130. 
131.         private void TogglePlay()
132.         { 
133.             //Toggle playback
134.             if (graphButton.Content.Equals("Start Graph"))
135.             {
136.                 graph.Start();
137.                 graphButton.Content = "Stop Graph";
138.                 audioPipe.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Blue);
139.             }
140.             else
141.             {
142.                 graph.Stop();
143.                 graphButton.Content = "Start Graph";
144.                 audioPipe.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 49, 49, 49));
145.             }
146.         }
147. 
148.         private void PlaySpeedSlider_ValueChanged(object sender, RangeBaseValueChangedEventArgs e)
149.         {
150.             if (fileInput != null)
151.             {
152.                 fileInput.PlaybackSpeedFactor = playSpeedSlider.Value;
153.             }
154.         }
155. 
156.         private void LoopToggle_Toggled(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
157.         {
158.             // Set loop count to null for infinite looping
159.             // Set loop count to 0 to stop looping after current iteration
160.             // Set loop count to non-zero value for finite looping
161.             if (loopToggle.IsOn)
162.             {
163.                 // If turning on looping, make sure the file hasn't finished playback yet
164.                 if (fileInput.Position >= fileInput.Duration)
165.                 {
166.                     // If finished playback, seek back to the start time we set
167.                     fileInput.Seek(fileInput.StartTime.Value);
168.                 }
169.                 fileInput.LoopCount = null; // infinite looping
170.             }
171.             else
172.             {
173.                 fileInput.LoopCount = 0; // stop looping
174.             }
175.         }
176. 
177.         private async Task CreateAudioGraph()
178.         {
179.             // Create an AudioGraph with default settings
180.             AudioGraphSettings settings = new AudioGraphSettings(AudioRenderCategory.Media);
181.             CreateAudioGraphResult result = await AudioGraph.CreateAsync(settings);
182. 
183.             if (result.Status != AudioGraphCreationStatus.Success)
184.             {
185.                 // Cannot create graph
186.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("AudioGraph Creation Error because {0}", result.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
187.                 return;
188.             }
189. 
190.             graph = result.Graph;
191. 
192.             // Create a device output node
193.             CreateAudioDeviceOutputNodeResult deviceOutputNodeResult = await graph.CreateDeviceOutputNodeAsync();
194. 
195.             if (deviceOutputNodeResult.Status != AudioDeviceNodeCreationStatus.Success)
196.             {
197.                 // Cannot create device output node
198.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("Device Output unavailable because {0}", deviceOutputNodeResult.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
199.                 speakerContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Red);
200.                 return;
201.             }
202. 
203.             deviceOutput = deviceOutputNodeResult.DeviceOutputNode;
204.             rootPage.NotifyUser("Device Output Node successfully created", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
205.             speakerContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Green);
206.         }
207.     }
208. }

84.         private async Task SelectOutputFile()
85.         {
86.             // TODO[migrate-002]: see PATTERNS.md#pickers
87.             FileSavePicker saveFilePicker = new FileSavePicker();
88.             saveFilePicker.FileTypeChoices.Add("Pulse Code Modulation", new List<string>() { ".wav" });
89.             saveFilePicker.FileTypeChoices.Add("Windows Media Audio", new List<string>() { ".wma" });
90.             saveFilePicker.FileTypeChoices.Add("MPEG Audio Layer-3", new List<string>() { ".mp3" });
91.             saveFilePicker.SuggestedFileName = "New Audio Track";
92.             StorageFile file = await saveFilePicker.PickSaveFileAsync();
93. 
94.             // File can be null if cancel is hit in the file picker
95.             if (file == null)
96.             {
97.                 return;
98.             }
99. 
100.             rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("Recording to {0}", file.Name.ToString()), NotifyType.StatusMessage);
101.             MediaEncodingProfile fileProfile = CreateMediaEncodingProfile(file);
102. 
103.             // Operate node at the graph format, but save file at the specified format
104.             CreateAudioFileOutputNodeResult fileOutputNodeResult = await graph.CreateFileOutputNodeAsync(file, fileProfile);
105. 
106.             if (fileOutputNodeResult.Status != AudioFileNodeCreationStatus.Success)
107.             {
108.                 // FileOutputNode creation failed
109.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("Cannot create output file because {0}", fileOutputNodeResult.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
110.                 fileButton.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Red);
111.                 return;
112.             }
113. 
114.             fileOutputNode = fileOutputNodeResult.FileOutputNode;
115.             fileButton.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.YellowGreen);
116. 
117.             // Connect the input node to both output nodes
118.             deviceInputNode.AddOutgoingConnection(fileOutputNode);
119.             deviceInputNode.AddOutgoingConnection(deviceOutputNode);
120.             recordStopButton.IsEnabled = true;
121.         }
122. 
123.         private MediaEncodingProfile CreateMediaEncodingProfile(StorageFile file)
124.         {
125.             switch(file.FileType.ToString().ToLowerInvariant())
126.             {
127.                 case ".wma":
128.                     return MediaEncodingProfile.CreateWma(AudioEncodingQuality.High);
129.                 case ".mp3":
130.                     return MediaEncodingProfile.CreateMp3(AudioEncodingQuality.High);
131.                 case ".wav":
132.                     return MediaEncodingProfile.CreateWav(AudioEncodingQuality.High);
133.                 default:
134.                     throw new ArgumentException();
135.             }
136.         }
137. 
138.         private async Task ToggleRecordStop()
139.         {
140.             if (recordStopButton.Content.Equals("Record"))
141.             {
142.                 graph.Start();
143.                 recordStopButton.Content = "Stop";
144.                 audioPipe1.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Blue);
145.                 audioPipe2.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Blue);
146.             }
147.             else if (recordStopButton.Content.Equals("Stop"))
148.             {
149.                 // Good idea to stop the graph to avoid data loss
150.                 graph.Stop();
151.                 audioPipe1.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 49, 49, 49));
152.                 audioPipe2.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 49, 49, 49));
153. 
154.                 TranscodeFailureReason finalizeResult = await fileOutputNode.FinalizeAsync();
155.                 if (finalizeResult != TranscodeFailureReason.None)
156.                 {
157.                     // Finalization of file failed. Check result code to see why
158.                     rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("Finalization of file failed because {0}", finalizeResult.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
159.                     fileButton.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Red);
160.                     return;
161.                 }
162. 
163.                 recordStopButton.Content = "Record";
164.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("Recording to file completed successfully!", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
165.                 fileButton.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Green);
166.                 recordStopButton.IsEnabled = false;
167.                 createGraphButton.IsEnabled = false;
168.             }
169.         }

171.         private async Task PopulateOutputDeviceList()
172.         {
173.             outputDevicesListBox.Items.Clear();
174.             outputDevices = await DeviceInformation.FindAllAsync(MediaDevice.GetAudioRenderSelector());
175.             outputDevicesListBox.Items.Add("-- Pick output device --");
176.             foreach (var device in outputDevices)
177.             {
178.                 outputDevicesListBox.Items.Add(device.Name);
179.             }
180.         }

182.         private async Task PopulateInputDeviceList()
183.         {
184.             inputDevicesListBox.Items.Clear();
185.             inputDevices = await DeviceInformation.FindAllAsync(MediaDevice.GetAudioCaptureSelector());
186.             inputDevicesListBox.Items.Add("-- Pick input device --");
187.             foreach (var device in inputDevices)
188.             {
189.                 inputDevicesListBox.Items.Add(device.Name);
190.             }
191.         }

193.         private async Task CreateAudioGraph()
194.         {
195.             AudioGraphSettings settings = new AudioGraphSettings(AudioRenderCategory.Media);
196.             settings.QuantumSizeSelectionMode = QuantumSizeSelectionMode.LowestLatency;
197.             settings.PrimaryRenderDevice = outputDevices[outputDevicesListBox.SelectedIndex - 1];
198. 
199.             CreateAudioGraphResult result = await AudioGraph.CreateAsync(settings);
200. 
201.             if (result.Status != AudioGraphCreationStatus.Success)
202.             {
203.                 // Cannot create graph
204.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("AudioGraph Creation Error because {0}", result.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
205.                 return;
206.             }
207. 
208.             graph = result.Graph;
209.             rootPage.NotifyUser("Graph successfully created!", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
210. 
211.             // Create a device output node
212.             CreateAudioDeviceOutputNodeResult deviceOutputNodeResult = await graph.CreateDeviceOutputNodeAsync();
213.             if (deviceOutputNodeResult.Status != AudioDeviceNodeCreationStatus.Success)
214.             {
215.                 // Cannot create device output node
216.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("Audio Device Output unavailable because {0}", deviceOutputNodeResult.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
217.                 outputDeviceContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Red);
218.                 return;
219.             }
220. 
221.             deviceOutputNode = deviceOutputNodeResult.DeviceOutputNode;
222.             rootPage.NotifyUser("Device Output connection successfully created", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
223.             outputDeviceContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Green);
224. 
225.             // Create a device input node using the default audio input device
226.             CreateAudioDeviceInputNodeResult deviceInputNodeResult = await graph.CreateDeviceInputNodeAsync(MediaCategory.Other, graph.EncodingProperties, inputDevices[inputDevicesListBox.SelectedIndex - 1]);
227. 
228.             if (deviceInputNodeResult.Status != AudioDeviceNodeCreationStatus.Success)
229.             {
230.                 // Cannot create device input node
231.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("Audio Device Input unavailable because {0}", deviceInputNodeResult.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
232.                 inputDeviceContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Red);
233.                 return;
234.             }
235. 
236.             deviceInputNode = deviceInputNodeResult.DeviceInputNode;
237.             rootPage.NotifyUser("Device Input connection successfully created", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
238.             inputDeviceContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Green);
239. 
240.             // Since graph is successfully created, enable the button to select a file output
241.             fileButton.IsEnabled = true;
242. 
243.             // Disable the graph button to prevent accidental click
244.             createGraphButton.IsEnabled = false;
245. 
246.             // Because we are using lowest latency setting, we need to handle device disconnection errors
247.             graph.UnrecoverableErrorOccurred += Graph_UnrecoverableErrorOccurred;
248.         }
249. 
250.         private async void Graph_UnrecoverableErrorOccurred(AudioGraph sender, AudioGraphUnrecoverableErrorOccurredEventArgs args)
251.         {
252.             // Recreate the graph and all nodes when this happens
253.             // TODO[migrate-003]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
254.             await Dispatcher.RunAsync(Windows.UI.Core.CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, async () =>
255.             {
256.                 sender.Dispose();
257.                 // Re-query for devices
258.                 await PopulateOutputDeviceList();
259.                 await PopulateInputDeviceList();
260.                 // Reset UI
261.                 fileButton.IsEnabled = false;
262.                 recordStopButton.IsEnabled = false;
263.                 recordStopButton.Content = "Record";
264.                 outputDeviceContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 74, 74, 74));
265.                 audioPipe1.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 49, 49, 49));
266.                 audioPipe2.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 49, 49, 49));
267.             });
268.         }
269. 
270.         private void outputDevicesListBox_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
271.         {
272.             if (outputDevicesListBox.SelectedIndex == 0)
273.             {
274.                 createGraphButton.IsEnabled = false;
275.                 outputDevice.Foreground = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 110, 110, 110));
276.                 outputDeviceContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 74, 74, 74));
277.                 fileButton.IsEnabled = false;
278.                 fileButton.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 74, 74, 74));
279.                 inputDeviceContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 74, 74, 74));
280. 
281.                 // Destroy graph
282.                 if (graph != null)
283.                 {
284.                     graph.Dispose();
285.                     graph = null;
286.                 }
287.             }
288.             else
289.             {
290.                 createGraphButton.IsEnabled = true;
291.                 outputDevice.Foreground = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.White);
292.             }
293.         }
294. 
295.         private void inputDevicesListBox_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
296.         {
297.             if (inputDevicesListBox.SelectedIndex == 0)
298.             {
299.                 createGraphButton.IsEnabled = false;
300.                 fileButton.IsEnabled = false;
301.                 fileButton.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 74, 74, 74));
302.                 inputDeviceContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 74, 74, 74));
303. 
304.                 // Destroy graph
305.                 if (graph != null)
306.                 {
307.                     graph.Dispose();
308.                     graph = null;
309.                 }
310.             }
311.             else
312.             {
313.                 createGraphButton.IsEnabled = true;
314.             }
315.         }

60.         private async void File1_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
61.         {
62.             // If another file is already loaded into the FileInput node
63.             if (fileInputNode1 != null)
64.             {
65.                 // Release the file and dispose the contents of the node
66.                 fileInputNode1.Dispose();
67.                 // Stop playback since a new file is being loaded. Also reset the button UI
68.                 if (graphButton.Content.Equals("Stop Graph"))
69.                 {
70.                     TogglePlay();
71.                 }
72.             }
73. 
74.             // TODO[migrate-004]: see PATTERNS.md#pickers
75.             FileOpenPicker filePicker = new FileOpenPicker();
76.             filePicker.SuggestedStartLocation = PickerLocationId.MusicLibrary;
77.             filePicker.FileTypeFilter.Add(".mp3");
78.             filePicker.FileTypeFilter.Add(".wma");
79.             filePicker.FileTypeFilter.Add(".wav");
80.             filePicker.ViewMode = PickerViewMode.Thumbnail;
81.             StorageFile file1 = await filePicker.PickSingleFileAsync();
82. 
83.             // File can be null if cancel is hit in the file picker
84.             if (file1 == null)
85.             {
86.                 return;
87.             }
88.

90.             if (fileInputNodeResult.Status != AudioFileNodeCreationStatus.Success)
91.             {
92.                 // Cannot read input file
93.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("Can't read input file1 because {0}", fileInputNodeResult.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
94.                 return;
95.             }
96. 
97.             fileInputNode1 = fileInputNodeResult.FileInputNode;
98.             // Since we are going to play two files simultaneously, set outgoing gain to 0.5 to prevent clipping
99.             fileInputNode1.AddOutgoingConnection(submixNode, 0.5);
100. 
101.             // The graph might already be playing, so set up UI accordingly
102.             if (graphButton.Content.Equals("Stop Graph"))
103.             {
104.                 audioPipe1.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Blue);
105.             }
106.

108.             graphButton.IsEnabled = true;
109.             echoEffectToggle.IsEnabled = true;
110.             rootPage.NotifyUser("Loaded File 1", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
111.             fileButton1.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Green);
112.         }

171.         private void TogglePlay()
172.         {
173.             if (graphButton.Content.Equals("Start Graph"))
174.             {

176.                 graphButton.Content = "Stop Graph";
177.                 audioPipe3.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Blue);

179.                 {

183.                 {
184.                     audioPipe2.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Blue);
185.                 }
186.             }
187.             else if (graphButton.Content.Equals("Stop Graph"))
188.             {
189.                 graph.Stop();
190.                 graphButton.Content = "Start Graph";
191.                 audioPipe1.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 49, 49, 49));
192.                 audioPipe2.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 49, 49, 49));
193.                 audioPipe3.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 49, 49, 49));
194.             }

197.         private void EchoEffectToggle_Toggled(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
198.         {
199.             // Enable/Disable the echo effect
200.             // Also set the label for the UI
201.             if (echoEffectToggle.IsOn)
202.             {
203.                 submixNode.EnableEffectsByDefinition(echoEffect);
204.             }
205.             else
206.             {
207.                 submixNode.DisableEffectsByDefinition(echoEffect);
208.             }
209.         }

211.         private async Task CreateAudioGraph()
212.         {
213.             // Create an AudioGraph with default setting
214.             AudioGraphSettings settings = new AudioGraphSettings(AudioRenderCategory.Media);
215.             CreateAudioGraphResult result = await AudioGraph.CreateAsync(settings);
216. 
217.             if (result.Status != AudioGraphCreationStatus.Success)
218.             {
219.                 // Can't create the graph
220.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("AudioGraph Creation Error because {0}", result.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
221.                 return;
222.             }
223. 
224.             graph = result.Graph;
225. 
226.             // Create a device output node
227.             CreateAudioDeviceOutputNodeResult deviceOutputNodeResult = await graph.CreateDeviceOutputNodeAsync();
228. 
229.             if (deviceOutputNodeResult.Status != AudioDeviceNodeCreationStatus.Success)
230.             {
231.                 // Cannot create device output node
232.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("Audio Device Output unavailable because {0}", deviceOutputNodeResult.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
233.                 speakerContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Red);
234.                 return;
235.             }
236. 
237.             deviceOutputNode = deviceOutputNodeResult.DeviceOutputNode;
238.             rootPage.NotifyUser("Device Output Node successfully created", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
239.             speakerContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Green);
240. 
241.             submixNode = graph.CreateSubmixNode();
242.             submixNodeContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Green);
243.             submixNode.AddOutgoingConnection(deviceOutputNode);
244. 
245.             echoEffect = new EchoEffectDefinition(graph);
246.             echoEffect.WetDryMix = 0.7f;
247.             echoEffect.Feedback = 0.5f;
248.             echoEffect.Delay = 500.0f;
249.             submixNode.EffectDefinitions.Add(echoEffect);
250. 
251.             // Disable the effect in the beginning. Enable in response to user action (UI toggle switch)
252.             submixNode.DisableEffectsByDefinition(echoEffect);
253. 
254.             // All nodes can have an OutgoingGain property
255.             // Setting the gain on the Submix node attenuates the output of the node
256.             submixNode.OutgoingGain = 0.5;
257. 
258.             // Graph successfully created. Enable buttons to load files
259.             fileButton1.IsEnabled = true;
260.             fileButton2.IsEnabled = true;
261.         }
262.     }

63.         private async void File_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
64.         {
65.             // If another file is already loaded into the FileInput node
66.             if (fileInputNode != null)
67.             {
68.                 // Release the file and dispose the contents of the node
69.                 fileInputNode.Dispose();
70.                 // Stop playback since a new file is being loaded. Also reset the button UI
71.                 if (graphButton.Content.Equals("Stop Graph"))
72.                 {
73.                     TogglePlay();
74.                 }
75.             }
76. 
77.             // TODO[migrate-006]: see PATTERNS.md#pickers
78.             FileOpenPicker filePicker = new FileOpenPicker();
79.             filePicker.SuggestedStartLocation = PickerLocationId.MusicLibrary;
80.             filePicker.FileTypeFilter.Add(".mp3");
81.             filePicker.FileTypeFilter.Add(".wma");
82.             filePicker.FileTypeFilter.Add(".wav");
83.             filePicker.ViewMode = PickerViewMode.Thumbnail;
84.             StorageFile file = await filePicker.PickSingleFileAsync();
85. 
86.             // File can be null if cancel is hit in the file picker
87.             if (file == null)
88.             {
89.                 return;
90.             }
91. 
92.             CreateAudioFileInputNodeResult fileInputResult = await graph.CreateFileInputNodeAsync(file);
93.             if (fileInputResult.Status != AudioFileNodeCreationStatus.Success)
94.             {
95.                 // Error reading the input file
96.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("Can't read input file because {0}", fileInputResult.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
97.                 return;
98.             }
99. 
100.             // File loaded successfully. Enable these buttons in the UI
101.             graphButton.IsEnabled           = true;
102.             echoEffectToggle.IsEnabled      = true;
103.             reverbEffectToggle.IsEnabled    = true;
104.             limiterEffectToggle.IsEnabled   = true;
105.             eqToggle.IsEnabled              = true;
106. 
107.             fileInputNode = fileInputResult.FileInputNode;
108.             fileInputNode.AddOutgoingConnection(deviceOutputNode);
109. 
110.             rootPage.NotifyUser("Successfully loaded input file", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
111.             fileButton.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Green);
112. 
113.             // Create the four inbox effects
114.             CreateEchoEffect();
115.             CreateReverbEffect();
116.             CreateLimiterEffect();
117.             CreateEqEffect();
118.         }
119. 
120.         private void Graph_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
121.         {
122.             TogglePlay();
123.         }

125.         private void TogglePlay()
126.         {
127.             if (graphButton.Content.Equals("Start Graph"))
128.             {
129.                 graph.Start();
130.                 graphButton.Content = "Stop Graph";
131.                 audioPipe.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Blue);
132.             }
133.             else
134.             {
135.                 graph.Stop();
136.                 graphButton.Content = "Start Graph";
137.                 audioPipe.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 49, 49, 49));
138.             }
139.         }

141.         private void EchoEffectToggle_Toggled(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
142.         {
143.             // Enable/Disable the effect in the graph
144.             // Also enable/disable the associated UI for effect parameters
145.             if (echoEffectToggle.IsOn)
146.             {
147.                 echoSlider.IsEnabled = true;
148.                 echoLabel.Foreground = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.White);
149.                 fileInputNode.EnableEffectsByDefinition(echoEffectDefinition);
150.             }
151.             else
152.             {
153.                 echoSlider.IsEnabled = false;
154.                 echoLabel.Foreground = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255,74,74,74));
155.                 fileInputNode.DisableEffectsByDefinition(echoEffectDefinition);
156.             }
157.         }

254. 
255.         // Mapping the 0-100 scale of the slider to a value between the min and max gain
256.         private double ConvertRange(double value)
257.         {
258.             // These are the same values as the ones in xapofx.h
259.             const double fxeq_min_gain = 0.126;
260.             const double fxeq_max_gain = 7.94;
261. 
262.             double scale = (fxeq_max_gain - fxeq_min_gain)/100;
263.             return (fxeq_min_gain + ((value) * scale));
264.         }
265.

267.         private void Eq1Slider_ValueChanged(object sender, RangeBaseValueChangedEventArgs e)
268.         {

271.                 double currentValue = ConvertRange(eq1Slider.Value);
272.                 eqEffectDefinition.Bands[0].Gain = currentValue;
273.             }
274.         }

303.         private void CreateEchoEffect()
304.         {
305.             // create echo effect
306.             echoEffectDefinition = new EchoEffectDefinition(graph);
307. 
308.             // See the MSDN page for parameter explanations
309.             // http://msdn.microsoft.com/en-us/library/windows/desktop/microsoft.directx_sdk.xapofx.fxecho_parameters(v=vs.85).aspx
310.             echoEffectDefinition.WetDryMix    = 0.7f;
311.             echoEffectDefinition.Feedback     = 0.5f;
312.             echoEffectDefinition.Delay        = 500.0f;
313. 
314.             fileInputNode.EffectDefinitions.Add(echoEffectDefinition);
315.             fileInputNode.DisableEffectsByDefinition(echoEffectDefinition);
316.         }
317. 
318.         private void CreateReverbEffect()
319.         {
320.             // Create reverb effect
321.             reverbEffectDefinition = new ReverbEffectDefinition(graph);
322. 
323.             // See the MSDN page for parameter explanations
324.             // https://msdn.microsoft.com/en-us/library/windows/desktop/microsoft.directx_sdk.xaudio2.xaudio2fx_reverb_parameters(v=vs.85).aspx
325.             reverbEffectDefinition.WetDryMix          = 50;
326.             reverbEffectDefinition.ReflectionsDelay   = 120;
327.             reverbEffectDefinition.ReverbDelay        = 30;
328.             reverbEffectDefinition.RearDelay          = 3;
329.             reverbEffectDefinition.DecayTime          = 2;
330. 
331.             fileInputNode.EffectDefinitions.Add(reverbEffectDefinition);
332.             fileInputNode.DisableEffectsByDefinition(reverbEffectDefinition);
333.         }
334. 
335.         private void CreateLimiterEffect()
336.         {
337.             // Create limiter effect
338.             limiterEffectDefinition = new LimiterEffectDefinition(graph);
339. 
340.             limiterEffectDefinition.Loudness = 1000;
341.             limiterEffectDefinition.Release = 10;
342. 
343.             fileInputNode.EffectDefinitions.Add(limiterEffectDefinition);
344.             fileInputNode.DisableEffectsByDefinition(limiterEffectDefinition);
345.         }
346. 
347.         private void CreateEqEffect()
348.         {
349.             // See the MSDN page for parameter explanations
350.             // https://msdn.microsoft.com/en-us/library/windows/desktop/microsoft.directx_sdk.xapofx.fxeq_parameters(v=vs.85).aspx
351.             eqEffectDefinition = new EqualizerEffectDefinition(graph);
352.             eqEffectDefinition.Bands[0].FrequencyCenter   = 100.0f;
353.             eqEffectDefinition.Bands[0].Gain              = 4.033f;
354.             eqEffectDefinition.Bands[0].Bandwidth         = 1.5f;
355. 
356.             eqEffectDefinition.Bands[1].FrequencyCenter   = 900.0f;
357.             eqEffectDefinition.Bands[1].Gain              = 1.6888f;
358.             eqEffectDefinition.Bands[1].Bandwidth         = 1.5f;
359. 
360.             eqEffectDefinition.Bands[2].FrequencyCenter   = 5000.0f;
361.             eqEffectDefinition.Bands[2].Gain              = 2.4702f;
362.             eqEffectDefinition.Bands[2].Bandwidth         = 1.5f;
363. 
364.             eqEffectDefinition.Bands[3].FrequencyCenter   = 12000.0f;
365.             eqEffectDefinition.Bands[3].Gain              = 5.5958f;
366.             eqEffectDefinition.Bands[3].Bandwidth         = 2.0f;
367. 
368.             fileInputNode.EffectDefinitions.Add(eqEffectDefinition);
369.             fileInputNode.DisableEffectsByDefinition(eqEffectDefinition);
370.         }
371. 
372.         private async Task CreateAudioGraph()
373.         {
374.             // Create an AudioGraph with default settings
375.             AudioGraphSettings settings = new AudioGraphSettings(AudioRenderCategory.Media);
376.             CreateAudioGraphResult result = await AudioGraph.CreateAsync(settings);
377. 
378.             if (result.Status != AudioGraphCreationStatus.Success)
379.             {
380.                 // Cannot create graph
381.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("AudioGraph Creation Error because {0}", result.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
382.                 return;
383.             }
384. 
385.             graph = result.Graph;
386. 
387.             // Create a device output node
388.             CreateAudioDeviceOutputNodeResult outputDeviceNodeResult = await graph.CreateDeviceOutputNodeAsync();
389. 
390.             if (outputDeviceNodeResult.Status != AudioDeviceNodeCreationStatus.Success)
391.             {
392.                 // Cannot create device output node
393.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("Audio Device Output unavailable because {0}", outputDeviceNodeResult.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
394.                 speakerContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Red);
395.                 return;
396.             }
397. 
398.             deviceOutputNode = outputDeviceNodeResult.DeviceOutputNode;
399.             rootPage.NotifyUser("Device Output Node successfully created", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
400.             speakerContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Green);
401.         }
402.     }

66.         private async Task SelectInputFile()
67.         {
68.             // If another file is already loaded into the FileInput node
69.             if (fileInputNode != null)
70.             {
71.                 // Release the file and dispose the contents of the node
72.                 fileInputNode.Dispose();
73.                 // Stop playback since a new file is being loaded. Also reset the button UI
74.                 if (graphButton.Content.Equals("Stop Graph"))
75.                 {
76.                     TogglePlay();
77.                 }
78.             }
79. 
80.             // TODO[migrate-007]: see PATTERNS.md#pickers
81.             FileOpenPicker filePicker = new FileOpenPicker();
82.             filePicker.SuggestedStartLocation = PickerLocationId.MusicLibrary;
83.             filePicker.FileTypeFilter.Add(".mp3");
84.             filePicker.ViewMode = PickerViewMode.Thumbnail;
85.             StorageFile file = await filePicker.PickSingleFileAsync();
86. 
87.             // File can be null if cancel is hit in the file picker
88.             if (file == null)
89.             {
90.                 return;
91.             }
92. 
93.             CreateAudioFileInputNodeResult fileInputNodeResult = await graph.CreateFileInputNodeAsync(file);
94.             if (fileInputNodeResult.Status != AudioFileNodeCreationStatus.Success)
95.             {
96.                 // Cannot read file
97.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("Cannot read input file because {0}", fileInputNodeResult.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
98.                 return;
99.             }
100. 
101.             fileInputNode = fileInputNodeResult.FileInputNode;
102.             fileInputNode.AddOutgoingConnection(deviceOutputNode);
103.             fileButton.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Green);
104. 
105.             // Event Handler for file completion
106.             fileInputNode.FileCompleted += FileInput_FileCompleted;
107. 
108.             // Enable the button to start the graph
109.             graphButton.IsEnabled = true;
110. 
111.             // Create the custom effect and apply to the FileInput node
112.             AddCustomEffect();
113.         }

115.         private void Graph_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
116.         {
117.             TogglePlay();
118.         }
119. 
120.         private void TogglePlay()
121.         {
122.             // Toggle playback
123.             if (graphButton.Content.Equals("Start Graph"))
124.             {
125.                 graph.Start();
126.                 graphButton.Content = "Stop Graph";
127.                 audioPipe.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Blue);
128.             }
129.             else
130.             {
131.                 graph.Stop();
132.                 graphButton.Content = "Start Graph";
133.                 audioPipe.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 49, 49, 49));
134.             }
135.         }
136. 
137.         private async Task CreateAudioGraph()
138.         {
139.             // Create an AudioGraph with default settings
140.             AudioGraphSettings settings = new AudioGraphSettings(AudioRenderCategory.Media);
141.             CreateAudioGraphResult result = await AudioGraph.CreateAsync(settings);
142. 
143.             if (result.Status != AudioGraphCreationStatus.Success)
144.             {
145.                 // Cannot create graph
146.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("AudioGraph Creation Error because {0}", result.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
147.                 return;
148.             }
149. 
150.             graph = result.Graph;
151. 
152.             // Create a device output node
153.             CreateAudioDeviceOutputNodeResult deviceOutputResult = await graph.CreateDeviceOutputNodeAsync();
154. 
155.             if (deviceOutputResult.Status != AudioDeviceNodeCreationStatus.Success)
156.             {
157.                 // Cannot create device output
158.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("Audio Device Output unavailable because {0}", deviceOutputResult.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
159.                 speakerContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Red);
160.                 return;
161.             }
162. 
163.             deviceOutputNode = deviceOutputResult.DeviceOutputNode;
164.             rootPage.NotifyUser("Device Output Node successfully created", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
165.             speakerContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Green);
166.         }
167. 
168.         private void AddCustomEffect()
169.         {
170.             // Create a property set and add a property/value pair
171.             PropertySet echoProperties = new PropertySet();
172.             echoProperties.Add("Mix", 0.5f);
173. 
174.             // Instantiate the custom effect defined in the 'CustomEffect' project
175.             AudioEffectDefinition echoEffectDefinition = new AudioEffectDefinition(typeof(AudioEchoEffect).FullName, echoProperties);
176.             fileInputNode.EffectDefinitions.Add(echoEffectDefinition);
177.         }
178. 
179.         // Event handler for file completion event
180.         private async void FileInput_FileCompleted(AudioFileInputNode sender, object args)
181.         {
182.             // File playback is done. Stop the graph
183.             graph.Stop();
184. 
185.             // Reset the file input node so starting the graph will resume playback from beginning of the file
186.             sender.Reset();
187. 
188.             // TODO[migrate-008]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
189.             await Dispatcher.RunAsync(Windows.UI.Core.CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
190.             {
191.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("End of file reached", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
192.                 graphButton.Content = "Start Graph";
193.             });
194.         }
195.     }

<a id="pickers"></a>
## Pickers and Win32 SurfacesUWP pickers infer the active window. WinUI 3 desktop pickers must be initialized explicitly or `ShowAsync` throws `COMException`.```csharp
var picker = new FileOpenPicker();
picker.FileTypeFilter.Add(".txt");
var hwnd = WinRT.Interop.WindowNative.GetWindowHandle(App.MainWindow);
WinRT.Interop.InitializeWithWindow.Initialize(picker, hwnd);
var file = await picker.PickSingleFileAsync();
```Apply the same `InitializeWithWindow.Initialize(obj, hwnd)` pattern to `FolderPicker`, `FileSavePicker`, `DataTransferManager` (Share), `PrintManager`, and other UI surfaces that target a window.
<exited with exit code 0>
--- RESULT ---
<a id="threading"></a>
## Threading: CoreDispatcher → DispatcherQueueUWP (replace this):```csharp
await Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () => StatusText.Text = "Done");
```WinUI 3:```csharp
DispatcherQueue.TryEnqueue(() => StatusText.Text = "Done");
DispatcherQueue.TryEnqueue(DispatcherQueuePriority.High, () => ProgressBar.Value = 100);
```Cache the queue off the UI thread via `DispatcherQueue.GetForCurrentThread()`. UWP's ASTA reentrancy protection is gone — watch for reentrancy in async code that pumps messages. See the official [threading guide](https://learn.microsoft.com/windows/apps/windows-app-sdk/migrate-to-windows-app-sdk/guides/threading).
<exited with exit code 0>

12. using System;
13. using System.Collections.Generic;
14. using Windows.UI.Core;
15. using Windows.UI.Xaml;
16. using Windows.UI.Xaml.Automation.Peers;
17. using Windows.UI.Xaml.Controls;
18. using Windows.UI.Xaml.Data;
19. using Windows.UI.Xaml.Media;
20. using Windows.UI.Xaml.Navigation;
21. 
22. // The Blank Page item template is documented at http://go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkId=402352&clcid=0x409
23. 
24. namespace SDKTemplate
25. {
26.     /// <summary>
27.     /// An empty page that can be used on its own or navigated to within a Frame.
28.     /// </summary>
29.     public sealed partial class MainPage : Page
30.     {
31.         public static MainPage Current;
32. 
33.         public MainPage()
34.         {
35.             this.InitializeComponent();
36. 
37.             // This is a static public property that allows downstream pages to get a handle to the MainPage instance
38.             // in order to call methods that are in this class.
39.             Current = this;
40.             SampleTitle.Text = FEATURE_NAME;
41.         }
42. 
43.         protected override void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)
44.         {
45.             // Populate the scenario list from the SampleConfiguration.cs file
46.             var itemCollection = new List<Scenario>();
47.             int i = 1;
48.             foreach (Scenario s in scenarios)
49.             {
50.                 itemCollection.Add(new Scenario { Title = $"{i++}) {s.Title}", ClassType = s.ClassType });
51.             }
52.             ScenarioControl.ItemsSource = itemCollection;
53. 
54.             if (Window.Current.Bounds.Width < 640)
55.             {
56.                 ScenarioControl.SelectedIndex = -1;
57.             }
58.             else
59.             {
60.                 ScenarioControl.SelectedIndex = 0;
61.             }
62.         }
63. 
64.         /// <summary>
65.         /// Called whenever the user changes selection in the scenarios list.  This method will navigate to the respective
66.         /// sample scenario page.
67.         /// </summary>
68.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
69.         /// <param name="e"></param>
70.         private void ScenarioControl_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
71.         {
72.             // Clear the status block when navigating scenarios.
73.             NotifyUser(String.Empty, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
74. 
75.             ListBox scenarioListBox = sender as ListBox;
76.             Scenario s = scenarioListBox.SelectedItem as Scenario;
77.             if (s != null)
78.             {
79.                 ScenarioFrame.Navigate(s.ClassType);
80.                 if (Window.Current.Bounds.Width < 640)
81.                 {
82.                     Splitter.IsPaneOpen = false;
83.                 }
84.             }
85.         }
86. 
87.         public List<Scenario> Scenarios
88.         {
89.             get { return this.scenarios; }
90.         }
91. 
92.         /// <summary>
93.         /// Display a message to the user.
94.         /// This method may be called from any thread.
95.         /// </summary>
96.         /// <param name="strMessage"></param>
97.         /// <param name="type"></param>
98.         public void NotifyUser(string strMessage, NotifyType type)
99.         {
100.             // If called from the UI thread, then update immediately.
101.             // Otherwise, schedule a task on the UI thread to perform the update.
102.             if (Dispatcher.HasThreadAccess)
103.             {
104.                 UpdateStatus(strMessage, type);
105.             }
106.             else
107.             {
108.                 var task = Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () => UpdateStatus(strMessage, type));
109.             }
110.         }
111. 
112.         private void UpdateStatus(string strMessage, NotifyType type)
113.         {
114.             switch (type)
115.             {
116.                 case NotifyType.StatusMessage:
117.                     StatusBorder.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Windows.UI.Colors.Green);
118.                     break;
119.                 case NotifyType.ErrorMessage:
120.                     StatusBorder.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Windows.UI.Colors.Red);
121.                     break;
122.             }
123. 
124.             StatusBlock.Text = strMessage;
125. 
126.             // Collapse the StatusBlock if it has no text to conserve real estate.
127.             StatusBorder.Visibility = (StatusBlock.Text != String.Empty) ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;
128.             if (StatusBlock.Text != String.Empty)
129.             {
130.                 StatusBorder.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
131.                 StatusPanel.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
132.             }
133.             else
134.             {
135.                 StatusBorder.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
136.                 StatusPanel.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
137.             }
138. 
139. 			// Raise an event if necessary to enable a screen reader to announce the status update.
140. 			var peer = FrameworkElementAutomationPeer.FromElement(StatusBlock);
141. 			if (peer != null)
142. 			{
143. 				peer.RaiseAutomationEvent(AutomationEvents.LiveRegionChanged);
144. 			}
145. 		}
146. 
147.         async void Footer_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
148.         {
149.             await Windows.System.Launcher.LaunchUriAsync(new Uri(((HyperlinkButton)sender).Tag.ToString()));
150.         }
151. 
152.         private void Button_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
153.         {
154.             Splitter.IsPaneOpen = !Splitter.IsPaneOpen;
155.         }
156.     }
157.     public enum NotifyType
158.     {
159.         StatusMessage,
160.         ErrorMessage
161.     };
162. }

1. ﻿using System;
2. using System.Collections.Generic;
3. using Windows.Media.Effects;
4. using Windows.Foundation;
5. using Windows.Foundation.Collections;
6. using Windows.Media;
7. using Windows.Media.MediaProperties;
8. using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
9. 
10. namespace CustomEffect
11. {
12.     // Using the COM interface IMemoryBufferByteAccess allows us to access the underlying byte array in an AudioFrame
13.     [ComImport]
14.     [Guid("5B0D3235-4DBA-4D44-865E-8F1D0E4FD04D")]
15.     [InterfaceType(ComInterfaceType.InterfaceIsIUnknown)]
16.     unsafe interface IMemoryBufferByteAccess
17.     {
18.         void GetBuffer(out byte* buffer, out uint capacity);
19.     }
20. 
21.     public sealed class AudioEchoEffect : IBasicAudioEffect
22.     {
23.         private AudioEncodingProperties currentEncodingProperties;
24.         private List<AudioEncodingProperties> supportedEncodingProperties;
25. 
26.         private float[] echoBuffer;
27.         private int currentActiveSampleIndex;
28.         private IPropertySet propertySet;
29. 
30.         // Mix does not have a set - all updates should be done through the property set.
31.         private float Mix
32.         {
33.             get
34.             {
35.                 object val;
36.                 if (propertySet != null && propertySet.TryGetValue("Mix", out val))
37.                 {
38.                     return (float)val;
39.                 }
40.                 return .5f;
41.             }
42.         }
43. 
44.         public bool UseInputFrameForOutput { get { return false; } }
45. 
46.         // Set up constant members in the constructor
47.         public AudioEchoEffect()
48.         {
49.             // Support 44.1kHz and 48kHz mono float
50.             supportedEncodingProperties = new List<AudioEncodingProperties>();
51.             AudioEncodingProperties encodingProps1 = AudioEncodingProperties.CreatePcm(44100, 1, 32);
52.             encodingProps1.Subtype = MediaEncodingSubtypes.Float;
53.             AudioEncodingProperties encodingProps2 = AudioEncodingProperties.CreatePcm(48000, 1, 32);
54.             encodingProps2.Subtype = MediaEncodingSubtypes.Float;
55. 
56.             supportedEncodingProperties.Add(encodingProps1);
57.             supportedEncodingProperties.Add(encodingProps2);
58.         }
59.         
60.         public IReadOnlyList<AudioEncodingProperties> SupportedEncodingProperties
61.         {
62.             get
63.             {
64.                 return supportedEncodingProperties;
65.             }
66.         }
67. 
68.         public void SetEncodingProperties(AudioEncodingProperties encodingProperties)
69.         {
70.             currentEncodingProperties = encodingProperties;
71. 
72.             // Create and initialize the echo array
73.             echoBuffer = new float[encodingProperties.SampleRate]; // exactly one second delay
74.             currentActiveSampleIndex = 0;
75.         }
76. 
77.         unsafe public void ProcessFrame(ProcessAudioFrameContext context)
78.         {
79.             AudioFrame inputFrame = context.InputFrame;
80.             AudioFrame outputFrame = context.OutputFrame;
81. 
82.             using (AudioBuffer inputBuffer = inputFrame.LockBuffer(AudioBufferAccessMode.Read),
83.                                 outputBuffer = outputFrame.LockBuffer(AudioBufferAccessMode.Write))
84.             using (IMemoryBufferReference inputReference = inputBuffer.CreateReference(),
85.                                             outputReference = outputBuffer.CreateReference())
86.             {
87.                 byte* inputDataInBytes;
88.                 byte* outputDataInBytes;
89.                 uint inputCapacity;
90.                 uint outputCapacity;
91. 
92.                 ((IMemoryBufferByteAccess)inputReference).GetBuffer(out inputDataInBytes, out inputCapacity);
93.                 ((IMemoryBufferByteAccess)outputReference).GetBuffer(out outputDataInBytes, out outputCapacity);
94. 
95.                 float* inputDataInFloat = (float*)inputDataInBytes;
96.                 float* outputDataInFloat = (float*)outputDataInBytes;
97. 
98.                 float inputData;
99.                 float echoData;
100. 
101.                 // Process audio data
102.                 int dataInFloatLength = (int)inputBuffer.Length / sizeof(float);
103. 
104.                 for (int i = 0; i < dataInFloatLength; i++)
105.                 {
106.                     inputData = inputDataInFloat[i] * (1.0f - this.Mix);
107.                     echoData = echoBuffer[currentActiveSampleIndex] * this.Mix;
108.                     outputDataInFloat[i] = inputData + echoData;
109.                     echoBuffer[currentActiveSampleIndex] = inputDataInFloat[i];
110.                     currentActiveSampleIndex++;
111. 
112.                     if (currentActiveSampleIndex == echoBuffer.Length)
113.                     {
114.                         // Wrap around (after one second of samples)
115.                         currentActiveSampleIndex = 0;
116.                     }
117.                 }
118.             }
119.         }
120. 
121.         public void Close(MediaEffectClosedReason reason)
122.         {
123.             // Clean-up any effect resources
124.             // This effect doesn't care about close, so there's nothing to do
125.         }
126. 
127.         public void DiscardQueuedFrames()
128.         {
129.             // Reset contents of the samples buffer
130.             Array.Clear(echoBuffer, 0, echoBuffer.Length - 1);
131.             currentActiveSampleIndex = 0;
132.         }
133. 
134.         public void SetProperties(IPropertySet configuration)
135.         {
136.             this.propertySet = configuration;
137.         }
138.     }
139. }

30. namespace AudioCreation
31. {
32.     // We are initializing a COM interface for use within the namespace
33.     // This interface allows access to memory at the byte level which we need to populate audio data that is generated
34.     [ComImport]
35.     [Guid("5B0D3235-4DBA-4D44-865E-8F1D0E4FD04D")]
36.     [InterfaceType(ComInterfaceType.InterfaceIsIUnknown)]
37. 
38.     unsafe interface IMemoryBufferByteAccess
39.     {
40.         void GetBuffer(out byte* buffer, out uint capacity);
41.     }
42. 
43.     /// <summary>
44.     /// Scenario 3: Using a frame input node to play audio. Audio data is dynamically generated to populate the audio frame
45.     /// </summary>
46.     public sealed partial class Scenario3_FrameInput : Page
47.     {
48.         private MainPage rootPage;
49.         private AudioGraph graph;
50.         private AudioDeviceOutputNode deviceOutputNode;
51.         private AudioFrameInputNode frameInputNode;
52.         public double theta = 0;
53. 
54.         public Scenario3_FrameInput()
55.         {
56.             this.InitializeComponent();
57.         }
58. 
59.         protected override async void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)
60.         {
61.             rootPage = MainPage.Current;
62.             await CreateAudioGraph();
63.         }
64. 
65.         protected override void OnNavigatedFrom(NavigationEventArgs e)
66.         {
67.             if (graph != null)
68.             {
69.                 graph.Dispose();
70.             }
71.         }
72. 
73.         private void Button_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
74.         {
75.             if (generateButton.Content.Equals("Generate Audio"))
76.             {
77.                 frameInputNode.Start();
78.                 generateButton.Content = "Stop";
79.                 audioPipe.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Blue);
80.             }
81.             else if (generateButton.Content.Equals("Stop"))
82.             {
83.                 frameInputNode.Stop();
84.                 generateButton.Content = "Generate Audio";
85.                 audioPipe.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 49, 49, 49));
86.             }
87.         }
88. 
89.         unsafe private AudioFrame GenerateAudioData(uint samples)
90.         {
91.             // Buffer size is (number of samples) * (size of each sample)
92.             // We choose to generate single channel (mono) audio. For multi-channel, multiply by number of channels
93.             uint bufferSize = samples * sizeof(float);
94.             AudioFrame frame = new Windows.Media.AudioFrame(bufferSize);
95. 
96.             using (AudioBuffer buffer = frame.LockBuffer(AudioBufferAccessMode.Write))
97.             using (IMemoryBufferReference reference = buffer.CreateReference())
98.             {
99.                 byte* dataInBytes;
100.                 uint capacityInBytes;
101.                 float* dataInFloat;
102. 
103.                 // Get the buffer from the AudioFrame
104.                 ((IMemoryBufferByteAccess)reference).GetBuffer(out dataInBytes, out capacityInBytes);
105. 
106.                 // Cast to float since the data we are generating is float
107.                 dataInFloat = (float*)dataInBytes;
108. 
109.                 float freq = 1000; // choosing to generate frequency of 1kHz
110.                 float amplitude = 0.3f;
111.                 int sampleRate = (int)graph.EncodingProperties.SampleRate;
112.                 double sampleIncrement = (freq * (Math.PI * 2)) / sampleRate;
113. 
114.                 // Generate a 1kHz sine wave and populate the values in the memory buffer
115.                 for (int i = 0; i < samples; i++)
116.                 {
117.                     double sinValue = amplitude * Math.Sin(theta);
118.                     dataInFloat[i] = (float)sinValue;
119.                     theta += sampleIncrement;
120.                 }
121.             }
122. 
123.             return frame;
124.         }
125. 
126.         private async Task CreateAudioGraph()
127.         {
128.             // Create an AudioGraph with default settings
129.             AudioGraphSettings settings = new AudioGraphSettings(AudioRenderCategory.Media);
130.             CreateAudioGraphResult result = await AudioGraph.CreateAsync(settings);
131. 
132.             if (result.Status != AudioGraphCreationStatus.Success)
133.             {
134.                 // Cannot create graph
135.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("AudioGraph Creation Error because {0}", result.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
136.                 return;
137.             }
138. 
139.             graph = result.Graph;
140. 
141.             // Create a device output node
142.             CreateAudioDeviceOutputNodeResult deviceOutputNodeResult = await graph.CreateDeviceOutputNodeAsync();
143.             if (deviceOutputNodeResult.Status != AudioDeviceNodeCreationStatus.Success)
144.             {
145.                 // Cannot create device output node
146.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("Audio Device Output unavailable because {0}", deviceOutputNodeResult.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
147.                 speakerContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Red);
148.             }
149. 
150.             deviceOutputNode = deviceOutputNodeResult.DeviceOutputNode;
151.             rootPage.NotifyUser("Device Output Node successfully created", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
152.             speakerContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Green);
153. 
154.             // Create the FrameInputNode at the same format as the graph, except explicitly set mono.
155.             AudioEncodingProperties nodeEncodingProperties = graph.EncodingProperties;
156.             nodeEncodingProperties.ChannelCount = 1;
157.             frameInputNode = graph.CreateFrameInputNode(nodeEncodingProperties);
158.             frameInputNode.AddOutgoingConnection(deviceOutputNode);
159.             frameContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Green);
160. 
161.             // Initialize the Frame Input Node in the stopped state
162.             frameInputNode.Stop();
163. 
164.             // Hook up an event handler so we can start generating samples when needed
165.             // This event is triggered when the node is required to provide data
166.             frameInputNode.QuantumStarted += node_QuantumStarted;
167.             
168.             // Start the graph since we will only start/stop the frame input node
169.             graph.Start();
170.         }
171. 
172.         private void node_QuantumStarted(AudioFrameInputNode sender, FrameInputNodeQuantumStartedEventArgs args)
173.         {
174.             // GenerateAudioData can provide PCM audio data by directly synthesizing it or reading from a file.
175.             // Need to know how many samples are required. In this case, the node is running at the same rate as the rest of the graph
176.             // For minimum latency, only provide the required amount of samples. Extra samples will introduce additional latency.
177.             uint numSamplesNeeded = (uint) args.RequiredSamples;
178. 
179.             if(numSamplesNeeded != 0)
180.             {
181.                 AudioFrame audioData = GenerateAudioData(numSamplesNeeded);
182.                 frameInputNode.AddFrame(audioData);
183.             }
184.         }
185.     }
186. }

Current { get; private set; }\n\n    public MainPage()\n    {\n        InitializeComponent();\n        Current = this;\n        SampleTitle.Text = FEATURE_NAME;\n\n        var items = new List<Scenario>();\n        int i = 1;\n        foreach (Scenario s in scenarios)\n        {\n            items.Add(new Scenario { Title = $\"{i++}) {s.Title}\", ClassType = s.ClassType });\n        }\n        ScenarioControl.ItemsSource = items;\n        ScenarioControl.SelectedIndex = 0;\n    }\n\n    private void ScenarioControl_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)\n    {\n        NotifyUser(string.Empty, NotifyType.StatusMessage);\n        ListBox scenarioListBox = (ListBox)sender;\n        Scenario? s = scenarioListBox.SelectedItem as Scenario;\n        if (s != null)\n        {\n            ScenarioFrame.Navigate(s.ClassType);\n        }\n    }\n\n    public void NotifyUser(string strMessage, NotifyType type)\n    {\n        if (DispatcherQueue.HasThreadAccess)\n        {\n            UpdateStatus(strMessage, type);\n        }\n        else\n        {\n            DispatcherQueue.TryEnqueue(() => UpdateStatus(strMessage, type));\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void UpdateStatus(string strMessage, NotifyType type)\n    {\n        switch (type)\n        {\n            case NotifyType.StatusMessage:\n                StatusBorder.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Green);\n                break;\n            case NotifyType.ErrorMessage:\n                StatusBorder.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Red);\n                break;\n        }\n\n        StatusBlock.Text = strMessage;\n        if (StatusBlock.Text != string.Empty)\n        {\n            StatusBorder.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;\n            StatusPanel.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;\n        }\n        else\n        {\n            StatusBorder.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;\n            StatusPanel.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\npublic enum NotifyType\n{\n    StatusMessage,\n    ErrorMessage\n}\n```\n\n### 8. 30.             new Scenario() { Title="Custom Effects", ClassType=typeof(Scenario6_CustomEffects)}
31.         };
32.     }
33. 
34.     public class Scenario
35.     {
36.         public string Title { get; set; }
37.         public Type ClassType { get; set; }
38.     }
39. }

30.     /// <summary>
31.     /// Using the AudioGraph API to playback from a file input.
32.     /// </summary>
33.     public sealed partial class Scenario1_FilePlayback : Page
34.     {
35.         private MainPage rootPage;
36. 
37.         private AudioGraph graph;
38.         private AudioFileInputNode fileInput;
39.         private AudioDeviceOutputNode deviceOutput;
40. 
41.         public Scenario1_FilePlayback()
42.         {
43.             this.InitializeComponent();
44.         }
45. 
46.         protected override async void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)
47.         {
48.             rootPage = MainPage.Current;
49.             await CreateAudioGraph();
50.         }
51. 
52.         protected override void OnNavigatedFrom(NavigationEventArgs e)
53.         {
54.             // Destroy the graph if the page is naviated away from
55.             if (graph != null)
56.             {
57.                 graph.Dispose();
58.             }
59.         }
60. 
61.         private async void File_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
62.         {
63.             // Clear any old messages.
64.             rootPage.NotifyUser("", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
65. 
66.             // If another file is already loaded into the FileInput node
67.             if (fileInput != null)
68.             {
69.                 // Release the file and dispose the contents of the node
70.                 fileInput.Dispose();
71.                 // Stop playback since a new file is being loaded. Also reset the button UI
72.                 if (graphButton.Content.Equals("Stop Graph"))
73.                 {
74.                     TogglePlay();
75.                 }
76.             }
77. 
78.             // TODO[migrate-001]: see PATTERNS.md#pickers
79.             FileOpenPicker filePicker = new FileOpenPicker();
80.             filePicker.SuggestedStartLocation = PickerLocationId.MusicLibrary;
81.             filePicker.FileTypeFilter.Add(".mp3");
82.             filePicker.FileTypeFilter.Add(".wav");
83.             filePicker.FileTypeFilter.Add(".wma");
84.             filePicker.FileTypeFilter.Add(".m4a");
85.             filePicker.ViewMode = PickerViewMode.Thumbnail;
86.             StorageFile file = await filePicker.PickSingleFileAsync();
87. 
88.             // File can be null if cancel is hit in the file picker
89.             if(file == null)
90.             {
91.                 return;
92.             }
93. 
94.             CreateAudioFileInputNodeResult fileInputResult = await graph.CreateFileInputNodeAsync(file);
95.             if (AudioFileNodeCreationStatus.Success != fileInputResult.Status)
96.             {
97.                 // Cannot read input file
98.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("Cannot read input file because {0}", fileInputResult.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
99.                 return;
100.             }
101. 
102.             fileInput = fileInputResult.FileInputNode;
103. 
104.             if (fileInput.Duration <= TimeSpan.FromSeconds(3))
105.             {
106.                 // Imported file is too short
107.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("Please pick an audio file which is longer than 3 seconds", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
108.                 fileInput.Dispose();
109.                 fileInput = null;
110.                 return;
111.             }
112. 
113.             fileInput.AddOutgoingConnection(deviceOutput);
114.             fileButton.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Green);
115. 
116.             // Trim the file: set the start time to 3 seconds from the beginning
117.             // fileInput.EndTime can be used to trim from the end of file
118.             fileInput.StartTime = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(3);
119. 
120.             // Enable buttons in UI to start graph, loop and change playback speed factor
121.             graphButton.IsEnabled = true;
122.             loopToggle.IsEnabled = true;
123.             playSpeedSlider.IsEnabled = true;
124.         }
125. 
126.         private void Graph_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
127.         {
128.             TogglePlay();
129.         }
130. 
131.         private void TogglePlay()
132.         { 
133.             //Toggle playback
134.             if (graphButton.Content.Equals("Start Graph"))
135.             {
136.                 graph.Start();
137.                 graphButton.Content = "Stop Graph";
138.                 audioPipe.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Blue);
139.             }
140.             else
141.             {
142.                 graph.Stop();
143.                 graphButton.Content = "Start Graph";
144.                 audioPipe.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 49, 49, 49));
145.             }
146.         }
147. 
148.         private void PlaySpeedSlider_ValueChanged(object sender, RangeBaseValueChangedEventArgs e)
149.         {
150.             if (fileInput != null)
151.             {
152.                 fileInput.PlaybackSpeedFactor = playSpeedSlider.Value;
153.             }
154.         }
155. 
156.         private void LoopToggle_Toggled(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
157.         {
158.             // Set loop count to null for infinite looping
159.             // Set loop count to 0 to stop looping after current iteration
160.             // Set loop count to non-zero value for finite looping
161.             if (loopToggle.IsOn)
162.             {
163.                 // If turning on looping, make sure the file hasn't finished playback yet
164.                 if (fileInput.Position >= fileInput.Duration)
165.                 {
166.                     // If finished playback, seek back to the start time we set
167.                     fileInput.Seek(fileInput.StartTime.Value);
168.                 }
169.                 fileInput.LoopCount = null; // infinite looping
170.             }
171.             else
172.             {
173.                 fileInput.LoopCount = 0; // stop looping
174.             }
175.         }
176. 
177.         private async Task CreateAudioGraph()
178.         {
179.             // Create an AudioGraph with default settings
180.             AudioGraphSettings settings = new AudioGraphSettings(AudioRenderCategory.Media);
181.             CreateAudioGraphResult result = await AudioGraph.CreateAsync(settings);
182. 
183.             if (result.Status != AudioGraphCreationStatus.Success)
184.             {
185.                 // Cannot create graph
186.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("AudioGraph Creation Error because {0}", result.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
187.                 return;
188.             }
189. 
190.             graph = result.Graph;
191. 
192.             // Create a device output node
193.             CreateAudioDeviceOutputNodeResult deviceOutputNodeResult = await graph.CreateDeviceOutputNodeAsync();
194. 
195.             if (deviceOutputNodeResult.Status != AudioDeviceNodeCreationStatus.Success)
196.             {
197.                 // Cannot create device output node
198.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("Device Output unavailable because {0}", deviceOutputNodeResult.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
199.                 speakerContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Red);
200.                 return;
201.             }
202. 
203.             deviceOutput = deviceOutputNodeResult.DeviceOutputNode;
204.             rootPage.NotifyUser("Device Output Node successfully created", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
205.             speakerContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Green);
206.         }
207.     }
208. }

36.     /// <summary>
37.     /// This scenario shows using AudioGraph for audio capture from a microphone with low latency.
38.     /// </summary>
39.     public sealed partial class Scenario2_DeviceCapture : Page
40.     {
41.         private MainPage rootPage;
42.         private AudioGraph graph;
43.         private AudioFileOutputNode fileOutputNode;
44.         private AudioDeviceOutputNode deviceOutputNode;
45.         private AudioDeviceInputNode deviceInputNode;
46.         private DeviceInformationCollection outputDevices;
47.         private DeviceInformationCollection inputDevices;
48. 
49.         public Scenario2_DeviceCapture()
50.         {
51.             this.InitializeComponent();
52.         }
53. 
54.         protected override async void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)
55.         {
56.             rootPage = MainPage.Current;
57.             await PopulateOutputDeviceList();
58.             await PopulateInputDeviceList();
59.         }
60. 
61.         protected override void OnNavigatedFrom(NavigationEventArgs e)
62.         {
63.             if (graph != null)
64.             {
65.                 graph.Dispose();
66.             }
67.         }

69.         private async void FileButton_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
70.         {
71.             await SelectOutputFile();
72.         }
73. 
74.         private async void RecordStopButton_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)

76.             await ToggleRecordStop();
77.         }

81.             await CreateAudioGraph();
82.         }
