84.         private async Task SelectOutputFile()
85.         {
86.             // TODO[migrate-002]: see PATTERNS.md#pickers
87.             FileSavePicker saveFilePicker = new FileSavePicker();
88.             saveFilePicker.FileTypeChoices.Add("Pulse Code Modulation", new List<string>() { ".wav" });
89.             saveFilePicker.FileTypeChoices.Add("Windows Media Audio", new List<string>() { ".wma" });
90.             saveFilePicker.FileTypeChoices.Add("MPEG Audio Layer-3", new List<string>() { ".mp3" });
91.             saveFilePicker.SuggestedFileName = "New Audio Track";
92.             StorageFile file = await saveFilePicker.PickSaveFileAsync();
93. 
94.             // File can be null if cancel is hit in the file picker
95.             if (file == null)
96.             {
97.                 return;
98.             }
99. 
100.             rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("Recording to {0}", file.Name.ToString()), NotifyType.StatusMessage);
101.             MediaEncodingProfile fileProfile = CreateMediaEncodingProfile(file);
102. 
103.             // Operate node at the graph format, but save file at the specified format
104.             CreateAudioFileOutputNodeResult fileOutputNodeResult = await graph.CreateFileOutputNodeAsync(file, fileProfile);
105. 
106.             if (fileOutputNodeResult.Status != AudioFileNodeCreationStatus.Success)
107.             {
108.                 // FileOutputNode creation failed
109.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("Cannot create output file because {0}", fileOutputNodeResult.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
110.                 fileButton.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Red);
111.                 return;
112.             }
113. 
114.             fileOutputNode = fileOutputNodeResult.FileOutputNode;
115.             fileButton.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.YellowGreen);
116. 
117.             // Connect the input node to both output nodes
118.             deviceInputNode.AddOutgoingConnection(fileOutputNode);
119.             deviceInputNode.AddOutgoingConnection(deviceOutputNode);
120.             recordStopButton.IsEnabled = true;
121.         }
122. 
123.         private MediaEncodingProfile CreateMediaEncodingProfile(StorageFile file)
124.         {
125.             switch(file.FileType.ToString().ToLowerInvariant())
126.             {
127.                 case ".wma":
128.                     return MediaEncodingProfile.CreateWma(AudioEncodingQuality.High);
129.                 case ".mp3":
130.                     return MediaEncodingProfile.CreateMp3(AudioEncodingQuality.High);
131.                 case ".wav":
132.                     return MediaEncodingProfile.CreateWav(AudioEncodingQuality.High);
133.                 default:
134.                     throw new ArgumentException();
135.             }
136.         }
137. 
138.         private async Task ToggleRecordStop()
139.         {
140.             if (recordStopButton.Content.Equals("Record"))
141.             {
142.                 graph.Start();
143.                 recordStopButton.Content = "Stop";
144.                 audioPipe1.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Blue);
145.                 audioPipe2.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Blue);
146.             }
147.             else if (recordStopButton.Content.Equals("Stop"))
148.             {
149.                 // Good idea to stop the graph to avoid data loss
150.                 graph.Stop();
151.                 audioPipe1.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 49, 49, 49));
152.                 audioPipe2.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 49, 49, 49));
153. 
154.                 TranscodeFailureReason finalizeResult = await fileOutputNode.FinalizeAsync();
155.                 if (finalizeResult != TranscodeFailureReason.None)
156.                 {
157.                     // Finalization of file failed. Check result code to see why
158.                     rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("Finalization of file failed because {0}", finalizeResult.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
159.                     fileButton.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Red);
160.                     return;
161.                 }
162. 
163.                 recordStopButton.Content = "Record";
164.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("Recording to file completed successfully!", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
165.                 fileButton.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Green);
166.                 recordStopButton.IsEnabled = false;
167.                 createGraphButton.IsEnabled = false;
168.             }
169.         }
170. 
171.         private async Task PopulateOutputDeviceList()
172.         {
173.             outputDevicesListBox.Items.Clear();
174.             outputDevices = await DeviceInformation.FindAllAsync(MediaDevice.GetAudioRenderSelector());
175.             outputDevicesListBox.Items.Add("-- Pick output device --");
176.             foreach (var device in outputDevices)
177.             {
178.                 outputDevicesListBox.Items.Add(device.Name);
179.             }
180.         }
181. 
182.         private async Task PopulateInputDeviceList()
183.         {
184.             inputDevicesListBox.Items.Clear();
185.             inputDevices = await DeviceInformation.FindAllAsync(MediaDevice.GetAudioCaptureSelector());
186.             inputDevicesListBox.Items.Add("-- Pick input device --");
187.             foreach (var device in inputDevices)
188.             {
189.                 inputDevicesListBox.Items.Add(device.Name);
190.             }
191.         }
192. 
193.         private async Task CreateAudioGraph()
194.         {
195.             AudioGraphSettings settings = new AudioGraphSettings(AudioRenderCategory.Media);
196.             settings.QuantumSizeSelectionMode = QuantumSizeSelectionMode.LowestLatency;
197.             settings.PrimaryRenderDevice = outputDevices[outputDevicesListBox.SelectedIndex - 1];
198. 
199.             CreateAudioGraphResult result = await AudioGraph.CreateAsync(settings);
200. 
201.             if (result.Status != AudioGraphCreationStatus.Success)
202.             {
203.                 // Cannot create graph
204.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("AudioGraph Creation Error because {0}", result.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
205.                 return;
206.             }
207. 
208.             graph = result.Graph;
209.             rootPage.NotifyUser("Graph successfully created!", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
210. 
211.             // Create a device output node
212.             CreateAudioDeviceOutputNodeResult deviceOutputNodeResult = await graph.CreateDeviceOutputNodeAsync();
213.             if (deviceOutputNodeResult.Status != AudioDeviceNodeCreationStatus.Success)
214.             {
215.                 // Cannot create device output node
216.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("Audio Device Output unavailable because {0}", deviceOutputNodeResult.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
217.                 outputDeviceContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Red);
218.                 return;
219.             }
220. 
221.             deviceOutputNode = deviceOutputNodeResult.DeviceOutputNode;
222.             rootPage.NotifyUser("Device Output connection successfully created", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
223.             outputDeviceContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Green);
224. 
225.             // Create a device input node using the default audio input device
226.             CreateAudioDeviceInputNodeResult deviceInputNodeResult = await graph.CreateDeviceInputNodeAsync(MediaCategory.Other, graph.EncodingProperties, inputDevices[inputDevicesListBox.SelectedIndex - 1]);
227. 
228.             if (deviceInputNodeResult.Status != AudioDeviceNodeCreationStatus.Success)
229.             {
230.                 // Cannot create device input node
231.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("Audio Device Input unavailable because {0}", deviceInputNodeResult.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
232.                 inputDeviceContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Red);
233.                 return;
234.             }
235. 
236.             deviceInputNode = deviceInputNodeResult.DeviceInputNode;
237.             rootPage.NotifyUser("Device Input connection successfully created", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
238.             inputDeviceContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Green);
239. 
240.             // Since graph is successfully created, enable the button to select a file output
241.             fileButton.IsEnabled = true;
242. 
243.             // Disable the graph button to prevent accidental click
244.             createGraphButton.IsEnabled = false;
245. 
246.             // Because we are using lowest latency setting, we need to handle device disconnection errors
247.             graph.UnrecoverableErrorOccurred += Graph_UnrecoverableErrorOccurred;
248.         }
249. 
250.         private async void Graph_UnrecoverableErrorOccurred(AudioGraph sender, AudioGraphUnrecoverableErrorOccurredEventArgs args)
251.         {
252.             // Recreate the graph and all nodes when this happens
253.             // TODO[migrate-003]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
254.             await Dispatcher.RunAsync(Windows.UI.Core.CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, async () =>
255.             {
256.                 sender.Dispose();
257.                 // Re-query for devices
258.                 await PopulateOutputDeviceList();
259.                 await PopulateInputDeviceList();
260.                 // Reset UI
261.                 fileButton.IsEnabled = false;
262.                 recordStopButton.IsEnabled = false;
263.                 recordStopButton.Content = "Record";
264.                 outputDeviceContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 74, 74, 74));
265.                 audioPipe1.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 49, 49, 49));
266.                 audioPipe2.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 49, 49, 49));
267.             });
268.         }
269. 
270.         private void outputDevicesListBox_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
271.         {
272.             if (outputDevicesListBox.SelectedIndex == 0)
273.             {
274.                 createGraphButton.IsEnabled = false;
275.                 outputDevice.Foreground = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 110, 110, 110));
276.                 outputDeviceContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 74, 74, 74));
277.                 fileButton.IsEnabled = false;
278.                 fileButton.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 74, 74, 74));
279.                 inputDeviceContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 74, 74, 74));
280. 
281.                 // Destroy graph
282.                 if (graph != null)
283.                 {
284.                     graph.Dispose();
285.                     graph = null;
286.                 }
287.             }
288.             else
289.             {
290.                 createGraphButton.IsEnabled = true;
291.                 outputDevice.Foreground = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.White);
292.             }
293.         }
294. 
295.         private void inputDevicesListBox_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
296.         {
297.             if (inputDevicesListBox.SelectedIndex == 0)
298.             {
299.                 createGraphButton.IsEnabled = false;
300.                 fileButton.IsEnabled = false;
301.                 fileButton.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 74, 74, 74));
302.                 inputDeviceContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 74, 74, 74));
303. 
304.                 // Destroy graph
305.                 if (graph != null)
306.                 {
307.                     graph.Dispose();
308.                     graph = null;
309.                 }
310.             }
311.             else
312.             {
313.                 createGraphButton.IsEnabled = true;
314.             }
315.         }

63.         private async void File_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)

75.             }
76. 
77.             // TODO[migrate-006]: see PATTERNS.md#pickers
78.             FileOpenPicker filePicker = new FileOpenPicker();
79.             filePicker.SuggestedStartLocation = PickerLocationId.MusicLibrary;
80.             filePicker.FileTypeFilter.Add(".mp3");
81.             filePicker.FileTypeFilter.Add(".wma");
82.             filePicker.FileTypeFilter.Add(".wav");
83.             filePicker.ViewMode = PickerViewMode.Thumbnail;
84.             StorageFile file = await filePicker.PickSingleFileAsync();
85. 
86.             // File can be null if cancel is hit in the file picker
87.             if (file == null)
88.             {
89.                 return;
90.             }
91. 
92.             CreateAudioFileInputNodeResult fileInputResult = await graph.CreateFileInputNodeAsync(file);
93.             if (fileInputResult.Status != AudioFileNodeCreationStatus.Success)
94.             {
95.                 // Error reading the input file
96.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("Can't read input file because {0}", fileInputResult.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
97.                 return;
98.             }
99. 
100.             // File loaded successfully. Enable these buttons in the UI
101.             graphButton.IsEnabled           = true;
102.             echoEffectToggle.IsEnabled      = true;
103.             reverbEffectToggle.IsEnabled    = true;
104.             limiterEffectToggle.IsEnabled   = true;
105.             eqToggle.IsEnabled              = true;
106. 
107.             fileInputNode = fileInputResult.FileInputNode;
108.             fileInputNode.AddOutgoingConnection(deviceOutputNode);
109. 
110.             rootPage.NotifyUser("Successfully loaded input file", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
111.             fileButton.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Green);
112. 
113.             // Create the four inbox effects
114.             CreateEchoEffect();
115.             CreateReverbEffect();
116.             CreateLimiterEffect();
117.             CreateEqEffect();
118.         }

255.         // Mapping the 0-100 scale of the slider to a value between the min and max gain
256.         private double ConvertRange(double value)
257.         {
258.             // These are the same values as the ones in xapofx.h
259.             const double fxeq_min_gain = 0.126;
260.             const double fxeq_max_gain = 7.94;
261. 
262.             double scale = (fxeq_max_gain - fxeq_min_gain)/100;
263.             return (fxeq_min_gain + ((value) * scale));
264.         }

372.         private async Task CreateAudioGraph()
373.         {
374.             // Create an AudioGraph with default settings
375.             AudioGraphSettings settings = new AudioGraphSettings(AudioRenderCategory.Media);
376.             CreateAudioGraphResult result = await AudioGraph.CreateAsync(settings);
377. 
378.             if (result.Status != AudioGraphCreationStatus.Success)
379.             {
380.                 // Cannot create graph
381.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("AudioGraph Creation Error because {0}", result.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
382.                 return;
383.             }
384. 
385.             graph = result.Graph;
386. 
387.             // Create a device output node
388.             CreateAudioDeviceOutputNodeResult outputDeviceNodeResult = await graph.CreateDeviceOutputNodeAsync();
389. 
390.             if (outputDeviceNodeResult.Status != AudioDeviceNodeCreationStatus.Success)
391.             {
392.                 // Cannot create device output node
393.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("Audio Device Output unavailable because {0}", outputDeviceNodeResult.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
394.                 speakerContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Red);
395.                 return;
396.             }
397. 
398.             deviceOutputNode = outputDeviceNodeResult.DeviceOutputNode;
399.             rootPage.NotifyUser("Device Output Node successfully created", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
400.             speakerContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Green);
401.         }
402.     }

14. using System.Runtime.InteropServices;

28. // The Blank Page item template is documented at http://go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkId=234238
29. 
30. namespace AudioCreation
31. {
32.     // We are initializing a COM interface for use within the namespace
33.     // This interface allows access to memory at the byte level which we need to populate audio data that is generated
34.     [ComImport]
35.     [Guid("5B0D3235-4DBA-4D44-865E-8F1D0E4FD04D")]
36.     [InterfaceType(ComInterfaceType.InterfaceIsIUnknown)]
37. 
38.     unsafe interface IMemoryBufferByteAccess
39.     {
40.         void GetBuffer(out byte* buffer, out uint capacity);
41.     }
42. 
43.     /// <summary>
44.     /// Scenario 3: Using a frame input node to play audio. Audio data is dynamically generated to populate the audio frame
45.     /// </summary>
46.     public sealed partial class Scenario3_FrameInput : Page
47.     {
48.         private MainPage rootPage;
49.         private AudioGraph graph;
50.         private AudioDeviceOutputNode deviceOutputNode;
51.         private AudioFrameInputNode frameInputNode;
52.         public double theta = 0;
53. 
54.         public Scenario3_FrameInput()
55.         {
56.             this.InitializeComponent();
57.         }
58. 
59.         protected override async void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)
60.         {
61.             rootPage = MainPage.Current;
62.             await CreateAudioGraph();
63.         }
64. 
65.         protected override void OnNavigatedFrom(NavigationEventArgs e)
66.         {
67.             if (graph != null)
68.             {
69.                 graph.Dispose();
70.             }
71.         }
72. 
73.         private void Button_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
74.         {
75.             if (generateButton.Content.Equals("Generate Audio"))
76.             {
77.                 frameInputNode.Start();
78.                 generateButton.Content = "Stop";
79.                 audioPipe.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Blue);
80.             }
81.             else if (generateButton.Content.Equals("Stop"))
82.             {
83.                 frameInputNode.Stop();
84.                 generateButton.Content = "Generate Audio";
85.                 audioPipe.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 49, 49, 49));
86.             }
87.         }
88. 
89.         unsafe private AudioFrame GenerateAudioData(uint samples)
90.         {
91.             // Buffer size is (number of samples) * (size of each sample)
92.             // We choose to generate single channel (mono) audio. For multi-channel, multiply by number of channels
93.             uint bufferSize = samples * sizeof(float);
94.             AudioFrame frame = new Windows.Media.AudioFrame(bufferSize);
95. 
96.             using (AudioBuffer buffer = frame.LockBuffer(AudioBufferAccessMode.Write))
97.             using (IMemoryBufferReference reference = buffer.CreateReference())
98.             {
99.                 byte* dataInBytes;
100.                 uint capacityInBytes;
101.                 float* dataInFloat;
102. 
103.                 // Get the buffer from the AudioFrame
104.                 ((IMemoryBufferByteAccess)reference).GetBuffer(out dataInBytes, out capacityInBytes);
105. 
106.                 // Cast to float since the data we are generating is float
107.                 dataInFloat = (float*)dataInBytes;
108. 
109.                 float freq = 1000; // choosing to generate frequency of 1kHz
110.                 float amplitude = 0.3f;
111.                 int sampleRate = (int)graph.EncodingProperties.SampleRate;
112.                 double sampleIncrement = (freq * (Math.PI * 2)) / sampleRate;
113. 
114.                 // Generate a 1kHz sine wave and populate the values in the memory buffer
115.                 for (int i = 0; i < samples; i++)
116.                 {
117.                     double sinValue = amplitude * Math.Sin(theta);
118.                     dataInFloat[i] = (float)sinValue;
119.                     theta += sampleIncrement;
120.                 }
121.             }
122. 
123.             return frame;
124.         }
125. 
126.         private async Task CreateAudioGraph()
127.         {
128.             // Create an AudioGraph with default settings
129.             AudioGraphSettings settings = new AudioGraphSettings(AudioRenderCategory.Media);
130.             CreateAudioGraphResult result = await AudioGraph.CreateAsync(settings);
131. 
132.             if (result.Status != AudioGraphCreationStatus.Success)
133.             {
134.                 // Cannot create graph
135.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("AudioGraph Creation Error because {0}", result.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
136.                 return;
137.             }
138. 
139.             graph = result.Graph;
140. 
141.             // Create a device output node
142.             CreateAudioDeviceOutputNodeResult deviceOutputNodeResult = await graph.CreateDeviceOutputNodeAsync();
143.             if (deviceOutputNodeResult.Status != AudioDeviceNodeCreationStatus.Success)
144.             {
145.                 // Cannot create device output node
146.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("Audio Device Output unavailable because {0}", deviceOutputNodeResult.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
147.                 speakerContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Red);
148.             }
149. 
150.             deviceOutputNode = deviceOutputNodeResult.DeviceOutputNode;
151.             rootPage.NotifyUser("Device Output Node successfully created", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
152.             speakerContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Green);
153. 
154.             // Create the FrameInputNode at the same format as the graph, except explicitly set mono.
155.             AudioEncodingProperties nodeEncodingProperties = graph.EncodingProperties;
156.             nodeEncodingProperties.ChannelCount = 1;
157.             frameInputNode = graph.CreateFrameInputNode(nodeEncodingProperties);
158.             frameInputNode.AddOutgoingConnection(deviceOutputNode);
159.             frameContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Green);
160. 
161.             // Initialize the Frame Input Node in the stopped state
162.             frameInputNode.Stop();
163. 
164.             // Hook up an event handler so we can start generating samples when needed
165.             // This event is triggered when the node is required to provide data
166.             frameInputNode.QuantumStarted += node_QuantumStarted;
167.             
168.             // Start the graph since we will only start/stop the frame input node
169.             graph.Start();
170.         }
171. 
172.         private void node_QuantumStarted(AudioFrameInputNode sender, FrameInputNodeQuantumStartedEventArgs args)
173.         {
174.             // GenerateAudioData can provide PCM audio data by directly synthesizing it or reading from a file.
175.             // Need to know how many samples are required. In this case, the node is running at the same rate as the rest of the graph
176.             // For minimum latency, only provide the required amount of samples. Extra samples will introduce additional latency.
177.             uint numSamplesNeeded = (uint) args.RequiredSamples;
178. 
179.             if(numSamplesNeeded != 0)
180.             {
181.                 AudioFrame audioData = GenerateAudioData(numSamplesNeeded);
182.                 frameInputNode.AddFrame(audioData);
183.             }
184.         }
185.     }
186. }

60.         private async void File1_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
61.         {
62.             // If another file is already loaded into the FileInput node
63.             if (fileInputNode1 != null)
64.             {
65.                 // Release the file and dispose the contents of the node
66.                 fileInputNode1.Dispose();
67.                 // Stop playback since a new file is being loaded. Also reset the button UI
68.                 if (graphButton.Content.Equals("Stop Graph"))
69.                 {
70.                     TogglePlay();
71.                 }
72.             }
73. 
74.             // TODO[migrate-004]: see PATTERNS.md#pickers
75.             FileOpenPicker filePicker = new FileOpenPicker();
76.             filePicker.SuggestedStartLocation = PickerLocationId.MusicLibrary;
77.             filePicker.FileTypeFilter.Add(".mp3");
78.             filePicker.FileTypeFilter.Add(".wma");
79.             filePicker.FileTypeFilter.Add(".wav");
80.             filePicker.ViewMode = PickerViewMode.Thumbnail;
81.             StorageFile file1 = await filePicker.PickSingleFileAsync();
82. 
83.             // File can be null if cancel is hit in the file picker
84.             if (file1 == null)
85.             {
86.                 return;
87.             }
88. 
89.             CreateAudioFileInputNodeResult fileInputNodeResult = await graph.CreateFileInputNodeAsync(file1);
90.             if (fileInputNodeResult.Status != AudioFileNodeCreationStatus.Success)
91.             {
92.                 // Cannot read input file
93.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("Can't read input file1 because {0}", fileInputNodeResult.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
94.                 return;
95.             }
96. 
97.             fileInputNode1 = fileInputNodeResult.FileInputNode;
98.             // Since we are going to play two files simultaneously, set outgoing gain to 0.5 to prevent clipping
99.             fileInputNode1.AddOutgoingConnection(submixNode, 0.5);
100. 
101.             // The graph might already be playing, so set up UI accordingly
102.             if (graphButton.Content.Equals("Stop Graph"))
103.             {
104.                 audioPipe1.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Blue);
105.             }
106. 
107.             // UI tasks: enable buttons, show status message and change color of the input node box
108.             graphButton.IsEnabled = true;
109.             echoEffectToggle.IsEnabled = true;
110.             rootPage.NotifyUser("Loaded File 1", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
111.             fileButton1.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Green);
112.         }
113. 
114.         private async void File2_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
115.         {
116.             // If another file is already loaded into the FileInput node
117.             if (fileInputNode2 != null)
118.             {
119.                 // Release the file and dispose the contents of the node
120.                 fileInputNode2.Dispose();
121.                 // Stop playback since a new file is being loaded. Also reset the button UI
122.                 if (graphButton.Content.Equals("Stop Graph"))
123.                 {
124.                     TogglePlay();
125.                 }
126.             }
127. 
128.             // TODO[migrate-005]: see PATTERNS.md#pickers
129.             FileOpenPicker filePicker = new FileOpenPicker();
130.             filePicker.SuggestedStartLocation = PickerLocationId.MusicLibrary;
131.             filePicker.FileTypeFilter.Add(".mp3");
132.             filePicker.ViewMode = PickerViewMode.Thumbnail;
133.             StorageFile file2 = await filePicker.PickSingleFileAsync();
134. 
135.             // File can be null if cancel is hit in the file picker
136.             if (file2 == null)
137.             {
138.                 return;
139.             }
140. 
141.             CreateAudioFileInputNodeResult fileInputNodeResult = await graph.CreateFileInputNodeAsync(file2);
142.             if (fileInputNodeResult.Status != AudioFileNodeCreationStatus.Success)
143.             {
144.                 // Cannot read input file
145.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("Can't read input file2 because {0}", fileInputNodeResult.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
146.                 return;
147.             }
148. 
149.             fileInputNode2 = fileInputNodeResult.FileInputNode;
150.             // Since we are going to play two files simultaneously, set outgoing gain to 0.5 to prevent clipping
151.             fileInputNode2.AddOutgoingConnection(submixNode, 0.5);
152. 
153.             // The graph might already be playing, so set up UI accordingly
154.             if (graphButton.Content.Equals("Stop Graph"))
155.             {
156.                 audioPipe2.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Blue);
157.             }
158. 
159.             // UI tasks: enable buttons, show status message and change color of the input node box
160.             graphButton.IsEnabled = true;
161.             echoEffectToggle.IsEnabled = true;
162.             rootPage.NotifyUser("Loaded File 2", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
163.             fileButton2.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Green);
164.         }
165. 
166.         private void GraphButton_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
167.         {
168.             TogglePlay();
169.         }
170. 
171.         private void TogglePlay()
172.         {
173.             if (graphButton.Content.Equals("Start Graph"))
174.             {
175.                 graph.Start();
176.                 graphButton.Content = "Stop Graph";
177.                 audioPipe3.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Blue);
178.                 if (fileInputNode1 != null)
179.                 {
180.                     audioPipe1.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Blue);
181.                 }
182.                 if (fileInputNode2 != null)
183.                 {
184.                     audioPipe2.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Blue);
185.                 }
186.             }
187.             else if (graphButton.Content.Equals("Stop Graph"))
188.             {
189.                 graph.Stop();
190.                 graphButton.Content = "Start Graph";
191.                 audioPipe1.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 49, 49, 49));
192.                 audioPipe2.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 49, 49, 49));
193.                 audioPipe3.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(255, 49, 49, 49));
194.             }
195.         }
196. 
197.         private void EchoEffectToggle_Toggled(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
198.         {
199.             // Enable/Disable the echo effect
200.             // Also set the label for the UI
201.             if (echoEffectToggle.IsOn)
202.             {
203.                 submixNode.EnableEffectsByDefinition(echoEffect);
204.             }
205.             else
206.             {
207.                 submixNode.DisableEffectsByDefinition(echoEffect);
208.             }
209.         }
210. 
211.         private async Task CreateAudioGraph()
212.         {
213.             // Create an AudioGraph with default setting
214.             AudioGraphSettings settings = new AudioGraphSettings(AudioRenderCategory.Media);
215.             CreateAudioGraphResult result = await AudioGraph.CreateAsync(settings);
216. 
217.             if (result.Status != AudioGraphCreationStatus.Success)
218.             {
219.                 // Can't create the graph
220.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("AudioGraph Creation Error because {0}", result.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
221.                 return;
222.             }
223. 
224.             graph = result.Graph;
225. 
226.             // Create a device output node
227.             CreateAudioDeviceOutputNodeResult deviceOutputNodeResult = await graph.CreateDeviceOutputNodeAsync();
228. 
229.             if (deviceOutputNodeResult.Status != AudioDeviceNodeCreationStatus.Success)
230.             {
231.                 // Cannot create device output node
232.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format("Audio Device Output unavailable because {0}", deviceOutputNodeResult.Status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
233.                 speakerContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Red);
234.                 return;
235.             }
236. 
237.             deviceOutputNode = deviceOutputNodeResult.DeviceOutputNode;
238.             rootPage.NotifyUser("Device Output Node successfully created", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
239.             speakerContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Green);
240. 
241.             submixNode = graph.CreateSubmixNode();
242.             submixNodeContainer.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Green);
243.             submixNode.AddOutgoingConnection(deviceOutputNode);
244. 
245.             echoEffect = new EchoEffectDefinition(graph);
246.             echoEffect.WetDryMix = 0.7f;
247.             echoEffect.Feedback = 0.5f;
248.             echoEffect.Delay = 500.0f;
249.             submixNode.EffectDefinitions.Add(echoEffect);
250. 
251.             // Disable the effect in the beginning. Enable in response to user action (UI toggle switch)
252.             submixNode.DisableEffectsByDefinition(echoEffect);
253. 
254.             // All nodes can have an OutgoingGain property
255.             // Setting the gain on the Submix node attenuates the output of the node
256.             submixNode.OutgoingGain = 0.5;
257. 
258.             // Graph successfully created. Enable buttons to load files
259.             fileButton1.IsEnabled = true;
260.             fileButton2.IsEnabled = true;
261.         }
262.     }
263. }

69.             if (fileInputNode != null)
70.             {
71.                 // Release the file and dispose the contents of the node
72.                 fileInputNode.Dispose();
73.                 // Stop playback since a new file is being loaded. Also reset the button UI

86.

88.             if (file == null)
89.             {
90.                 return;
91.             }
